[\[354a.1\]]lho phyogs kyi ri ban rgya'i rgyud las chu 'bab pa yang mdze dang skya rbab kyi nad dang [{]glad pa [,]klad pa [}]na bar 'gyur te/_chu de lta bu ni mi dge'o/_/nub phyogs na yul pa ri ya ta'i ri rgyud las chu 'bab pa ni nad kun sel ba'i chu ste bzang ngo /_/mdor na yul 
[\[354a.2\]]skam sa dang /_yul gsher sa ser che ba'i yul 'di gnyis kyi khron pa dang /_rdzing dang chu mig la sogs pa skyon dang yon tan gnyis ka yang skam sa dang gsher sa'i yon tan so sor bshad pa bzhin du mdor shes par bya'o/_/chu 'thung bar lobs pa'am/_skom 
[\[354a.3\]]ches te brnags te ma nus sam/_skom btung ba dang yang ma ldan na yang nyung shas tsam las mi btung ste/_yang na drod chung ngam/_skrang nad yod dam/_skya rbab kyi nad dam/_dmu rdzing dang /_gsus po che la sogs pa'i nad yod dam/_tshad pa na'am/_gzhang 
[\[354a.4\]]'brum mam/_pho ba drod chung ngam/_lus skams par gyur tam/_'or nad yod na chu 'thung du mi rung ngo /_/nad med pa'i mis ni so ga tsha ba dang /_ston ka'i dus su nyung shas tsam zhig 'thung du rung ngo /_/de la mi'i lus ran pa ran pa'am/_spom po'am/_skem por 
[\[354a.5\]]'gyur ba na zan phyed zos te chu 'thungs na phra spom ran par 'ong ngo /_/zan zos pa'i 'og tu chu 'thungs na lus sbom por 'ong ngo /_/sngar chu 'thungs la phyis zan zos na lus skem por 'ong /_/dmigs kyis chu 'thung du rung ba ni/_chang nad langs te mi bde na chu 
[\[354a.6\]]'thung du rung ngo /_/brgyal bar gyur pa la gdong du chu btab ste blud do/_/skyugs sam ngal te chad pa'am/_mgo 'khor ba'i nad la chu 'thungs na phan no/_/yang na skom mam lus tsha'am/_mkhris pa dang khrag rgyas sam/_khong pa dang lus tsha ches sam/_
[\[354a.7\]]dug 'thungs pa 'di lta bu la chu 'thungs na phan te dge'o/_/de la skom du chu 'thungs pa'i thabs ni/_/chu gdus te bzhi cha 'am/_sum cha gdu bar byas pa'i chu dron po khas bzod tsam du 'thungs na/_drod che zas 'ju mgul pa bde/_lus yang /_lgang pa'i 
[\[354b\]]
[\[354b.1\]]chu so min zhing bshang gci gnyis ka legs par 'ong ngo /_/skyigs bus btab pa la phan/_lto sbos pa la phan/_rlung nad dang bad kan rgyas pa dang /_rims gsar lud pa dang /_cham pa na ba dang /_dbugs mi bde ba dang /_rtsib logs su zug pa 'di 
[\[354b.2\]]rnams la phan te dge'o/_/de lta bu'i chu gdus pa bsgrangs te grang mor 'thungs pa ni bad kan gyi nad du yang mi 'gyur/_lus kyi khong yang mkhris pa'i nad tsha ba la yang phan te dge'o/_/chu gdus pa de zhag lon par gyur pa rnying pa ni rlung dang bad kan dang mkhris pa'i 
[\[354b.3\]]nad g.yo par 'gyur te mi dge'o/_/na li ke ra'i 'bras bu gdus pa'i khu ba'am/_chur sbangs pa'i skom gyi ro ni dbyar 'thungs na khong snum ro tsa bar 'gyur/_bsil yang /_skom pa la phan/_mkhris pa dang rlung nad sel/_pho ba'i drod skye/_lgang pa'i nad sbyong /_
[\[354b.4\]]bshang gci legs par 'byung ste dge'o/_/dbyar gyi char pa bab pa'i chu snod dam pa gzhi bzang por bsags pa ni bzang ste dge'o/_/dbyar gyi chu 'brub pa ljan ljin rnyog ma can dang 'dres pa ni ngan te btung du mi rung ngo /_/chu sna tshogs kyi bye brag bshad pa'o/_/
[\[354b.5\]]'o ma sna tshogs kyi bye brag bshad par bya'o/_/'o ma ni phal cher 'jam zhing mngar ba yin/_zhu ba'i rjes ni khong du snam/_mdangs bzang bar 'gyur ro/_/srog zungs bdun brtas zhing skye bar byed/_rlung dang mkhris pa'i nad sel/_khong brta zhing ro tsa/_bad kan 
[\[354b.6\]]skye/_'o ma 'ju dka' la bsil ba yin no/_/de la bye brag tu ba'i 'o ma ni 'thungs na 'tsho bar byed de bcud kyis len du 'gyur/_glo rdol ba dang /_glo gcong la phan/_yid gzhungs par 'gyur/_bzhin mdog bzang /_bud med kyi nu zho mchog tu skye bar 'gyur/_
[\[354b.7\]]ngal te chad pa dang /_nad kyis myos te brgyal ba dang 'dra bar mi dran pa dang /_cha nyams te bzhin mdog ngan cing mi sdug pa lta bur gyur pa dang /_dbugs mi bde ba dang /_lud pa lu ba dang /_rab tu skom pa dang /_bkres pa dang /_rims bying ba dang /_chu srib 
[\[355a\]]
[\[355a.1\]]dang /_khrag dang mkhris pa rgyas pa'i nad 'di rnams la phan no/_/ma he'i 'o ma ni pho ba'i me drod ha cang ches pas 'drangs mi shes pa'i nad/_gnyid med pa dang /_'di rnams la ma he'i 'o ma bsil la lci bas na nad 'di rnams la phan no/_/ra ni chu 
[\[355a.2\]]nyung 'thung gzas rgod/_/rtsi shing kha ba dang /_kha ba tshol zhing za/_khong yang bas ni de ltar ra'i 'o ma ni skem pa dang /_rims dang /_dbugs mi bde ba dang /_khrag mkhris rgyas pa'i nad dang /_tshad pa'i nad 'di rnams la phan no/_/rnga mo'i 'o ma ni cung zad rtsub cing tsha la 
[\[355a.3\]]lan tshwa bro ste/_'thungs na drod che/_khong yang /_bad kan dang rlung nad sel/_lto spos pa la phan/_khong na srin bu yod pa dang 'or nad dang /_dmu rdzing lta bu gsus pa cher skyes pa'i nad dang /_gzhang 'brum gyi nad 'di rnams la phan te dge'o/_/bud med kyi nu zho ni 
[\[355a.4\]]rlung nad dang /_mkhris pa'i nad la phan/_khrag nad dang /_mtshon gyis smas pa dang /_rma byung ba dang /_mig nad la phan/_nu zho 'thungs na byis pa la bsod cing ngoms par byed/_thigs pa mig tu blugs na mig la phan/_snar blugs na mtshul pa yan chad 
[\[355a.5\]]kyi nad kun la phan no/_/lug gi 'o ma ni snying la gnod/_tsha ba skye/_skyigs bu dang dbugs mi bde/_mkhris pa dang bad kan gyi nad du 'gyur/_rlung nad la ni phan no/_/ba glang mo'i 'o ma ni 'thungs na lus kyi khong rab tu tsha/_lus kyi sha skye zhing lus brtan par 
[\[355a.6\]]'gyur ro/_/rta lta bu rmig pa gcig pa'i 'o ma ni yang ste dpung pa dang /_lag ngar dang /_lag sor dang /_brla dang byin pa dang /_rkang sor yan chad rlung zhugs pa'i rlung nad la phan/_glo na ba la phan/_rta lta bu'i 'o ma ni skyur la lan tshwa bro ba yin te/_'phral du rmongs 
[\[355a.7\]]rmongs por 'gyur ro/_/de ltar 'o ma gang yang rung /_ma bskol bar 'o rlon 'thungs na bad kan du 'gyur/_lci ste 'ju dka'o/_/'o ma bskol ba ran par 'thungs na/_'ju sla ba bad kan du yang mi 'gyur ro/_/'o ma phyed cher gdus pa 'am/_sum cha'am/_bzhi 
[\[355b\]]
[\[355b.1\]]char gdus pa'i 'o ma ni 'ju dka' ste lci'o/_/'o ma bzhos ma thag gi dron po mod la 'thung ba ni bdud rtsi dang 'dra ste/_ci la yang phan zhing dge'o/_/zho'o cog ni 'thungs na zhu ba'i rjes skyur bar 'gyur/_rtug skam du 'gyur/_'khru ba gcod/_lci ste 'ju dka'/_tsha ba skye/_
[\[355b.2\]]rlung sel/_tshil skye/_khu chu mang du 'gyur/_nyams 'tshag skye/_stobs skye/_bad kan du 'gyur/_mkhris pa skye/_khrag brta/_zas 'ju ba'i me drod skye/_lus skrang ba'i bag tu 'gyur/_zho 'thungs na yi ga 'bye/_yi ga 'chus pa dang /_grang ba'i rims dang /_
[\[355b.3\]]rims ldang dub kyi dus mi mthun pa dang /_cham pa dang /_chu sri ba dang /_pho ba'i nang du rlung zhugs te rtsub por gyur pa 'di rnams la phan no/_/zho mngal dron po ni dpyid dang /_sos ka dang /_ston gyi dus tshigs 'di gsum gyi tshe tsha ba ste btung du mi rung ngo /_/zho bsgrangs 
[\[355b.4\]]pa grang mo ni btung du rung ngo /_/zho btung ba yang sbrang rtsi'am mar ram bu ram mam kha ra dkar po btab la btung ngo /_/yang na skyu ru ra btab la btung ngo /_/zho rtag par btung ba ni mi dge'o/_/mtshan mo btung ba ni mi dge'o/_/mon sran sde'u btsos pa'i khu bcud sbyar 
[\[355b.5\]]ba mi btung ngo /_/zho ma langs pa 'thungs na rims nad dang /_khrag nad dang /_mkhris nad dang /_me dbal dang /_mdze dang /_skya rbab dang /_mgo 'khor ba'i nad 'di rnams su 'gyur ro/_/dar ba sar pa ni yang la ro bska zhing skyur ba dang /_drod che ste bad kan dang /_
[\[355b.6\]]rlung nad sel/_skrang ba'i bag dang /_dmu rdzing dang /_gzhang 'brum khrag tshad du 'gyur pa'i nad dang /_gzhang 'brum dang /_pho ba'i nad dang /_chu sri ba dang /_yi ga 'chus pa dang /_skran nad dang /_mcher pa dang /_mar cher zos pa las nad du gyur pa dang /_dug 
[\[355b.7\]]sbyar ma dang /_skya rbab kyi nad dang /_'di rnams sel te phan par 'gyur ro/_/dar ba dang 'dra bar zho ga chu'i yon tan yang de bzhin te khong 'khru bar 'gyur/_lgang pa 'khris shing chu sri ba'am/_rtug skam lta bu sel zhing legs par 'byung ba dang /_khong yang bar 
[\[356a\]]
[\[356a.1\]]'gyur ro/_/mar sar phyung ma thag pa ni bud med la phod de ro tsa/_bsil/_sha mdog bzang /_stobs skye/_'khru ba gcod par byed/_rlung dang /_mkhris pa dang /_khrag nad dang /_glo gcong dang /_gzhang 'brum dang /_/ba yon por gyur pa dang /_
[\[356a.2\]]lud pa dang /_'di rnams sel te phan no/_/de la mar sar pa ni yid rno/_dran pa che/_yid gzhungs/_drod skye/_stobs skye/_tshe ring /_khu chu mang bar 'gyur ro/_/mig rno ba la phan no/_/zhun mar ni byis pa dang rgan po la phan te bu mang bar 'dod pa dang /_bzhin mdog 
[\[356a.3\]]bzang zhing mdangs can du 'dod dam/_rab tu gzhon par 'dod dam/_skad snyan par 'dod pa 'di lta bu la phan/_glo rdol dang /_lus kyi srog zungs bdun zad de skams pa gcong nad dang /_'bras dang /_mtshon gyis smas pa dang /_mes tshig ste rke zhing rmya ba la phan no/_/
[\[356a.4\]]rlung dang mkhris pa'i nad dang /_dug dang smyo ba'i nad dang /_lus skems pa dang /_lus dang bzhin gyi mdangs dang gzi nyams te brlangs brlangs por gyur pa dang /_rims nad sel te phan no/_/til mar dang tshil dang zhag dang mar dang /_snum 'di bzhi'i nang na zhun 
[\[356a.5\]]mar las bzang ba med de/_zhun mar ni tsha ba'i nad sel te bsil bar byed/_rgas sra ba la mchog tu phan/_zhun mar gyi mthu stobs ni sna grangs stong yod de/_de la yang sman dang sbyar ba'i sman mar zhun mar ni/_phan pa'i las stong byed nus par 'gyur ro/_/zhun mar rnying pa 
[\[356a.6\]]ni chang gis myos te ra ro ba dang /_rlung dang mkhris pa las gyur pa'i smyo ba'i nad dam/_gdon gyis myos pa 'am/_brgyal ba 'am/[ {]glad pa [,]klad pa [}]na ba 'am/_rna ba na ba 'am/_mig na ba 'am/_bud med kyi mngal na ba dang /_rma'i rnag dang /_chu ser 'byang zhing rma 'tshob pa 'di rnams 
[\[356a.7\]]la phan no/_/phrum gsar pa dang /_spri dang rgya phrum dang chur ba la sogs pa 'di rnams 'dra ste stobs skye/_/bu chu mang bar 'gyur/_gnyid 'ong /_bad kan du 'gyur/_rtug skam du 'gyur/_phrum dang 'di bzhi khar yang lci ste 'ju dka'/_rlung dang bad kan dang mkhris 
[\[356b\]]
[\[356b.1\]]pa'i nad du 'gyur ro/_/ba'i 'o ma dang zhun mar ni ci la yang dge ste/_mchog go/_/lug gi 'o ma dang mar ni ci la yang mi dge ste smad do/_/'o ma dang zho dang mar dang dar ba dang /_phrum la sogs pa bshad pa'i le'u rdzogs so//_//bu ram shing dang bu ram 
[\[356b.2\]]la sogs pa'i mthu dang yon tan bshad par bya'o/_/bu ram shing gi khu ba ni 'ju dka' ste lci/_khong 'khru/_khong du 'jam/_lus sha skye/_bud med la phod de ro tsa par 'gyur/_khong bsil/_bad kan skye/_gcin mang /_khrag dang mkhris pa sel te/_zhu ba'i rjes ni 
[\[356b.3\]]mngar bar 'gyur ro/_/bu ram shing gi thor to ni cung zad lan tshwa bro ba yin no/_/bu ram shing sos bca' zhing khu ba gzhibs pa ni li khar dang 'dra ste bzang ngo /_/bu ram shing brdungs pa ni rtsa ba dang thor to ni srin bus zos te mi bzang la bu ram shing gi shun pa dang /_la la 
[\[356b.4\]]skams pa dang /_rdul dang dri ma dang sa song ba nyi ma'i 'od dang /_rlung gis phog pa la sogs pa brdungs nas kyang cung zad gzhag gam/_zhag lon par gyur pa ni bu ram gyi ro las yid tsam skyur ba dang /_mi zhim par gyur pas na bu ram btsir ba'i khu ba ni mi dge ste lci la 
[\[356b.5\]]tsha bar 'gyur/_phyir 'byung du mi rung ste/_'ju dka' la 'khren pa dgos te/_de bas na bu ram brdungs te btsir ba'i khu ba ni mi bzang ngo /_/de la li kha ra dkar po'i shing ni dkar te khu ba ni ro mngar la bsil/_mig rno/_/rna ba gsang /_sems bde bar 'gyur ro/_/yang bu ram 
[\[356b.6\]]shing phal pa bang sha ka zhes bya ba bu ram shing snga ma tsam du mi bzang ba zhig yod pa de'i mthu dang yon tan dang skyon ni gong ma dang 'dra mod kyi phan yon gong ma tsam mi phod do/_/de la bu ram shing gi bye brag ni bu ram shing shata zhes bya ba gnag la sbom zhing ring ba dang /_bu ram shing ba 
[\[356b.7\]]pa ka zhes bya ba tshigs mang ba zhig yod pa dang /_bu ram shing ka tar zhes bya ba phre'u thung ngu zhig yod pa dang /_bal yul gyi bu ram shing 'di la sogs pa'i ro cung zad tsam bro ba dang /_ro bska ba dang /_zos na dro ba dang /_zhu ba'i rjes tsha bar 'gyur ro/_/bu ram shing 'di 
[\[357a\]]
[\[357a.1\]]bzhi yang ming gi [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin 'og ma rnams cung tsam gyis ro bro ba dang /_mthu'i shas chud do/_/de la bu ram gyi bye brag ni rnam pa gsum ste/_bu ram bskol te gdus pa'i che kha'i dbu ba dang dri ma ngan pa bzhams pa'i 'og dbu ba can kha spri sla 
[\[357a.2\]]mo zhig yod pa de ni 'ju dka' ste lci/_sla ba 'byar 'byar po yin te mchin pa'i nad dang bshang gci sla ba ma yin te/_rnyed par 'gyur ro/_/de la btsos shing gdus pa las bar nas byung pa ni bu ram zhes bya ste/_de la yang bu ram dkar po ni 'phral du bad kan du 'gyur ba ni ma 
[\[357a.3\]]yin no/_/bshang gci gnyis ka yang mi sri ste bde bar 'ong ngo /_/bu ram dmar po rtsa ba gnag pa ni phal cher srin bur 'gyur/_khrag dang tshil dang sha skye/_bad kan skye ste mi bzang ngo /_/bu ram dmar po lo gcig lon phan chad rnying pa zhes bya zhim ste yid du 'ong ngo /_/ci la 
[\[357a.4\]]yang phan no/_/rlung dang bad kan dang mkhris pa'i nad gsum char sel te dge'o/_/bu ram gsar pas ni bad kan skye/_pho ba'i me drod med par byed de mi dge'o/_/de ltar bu ram gyi khu ba gdus pa'i zhabs nas byung ba de ni li kha rar 'gyur ro/_/tshul 'di dang 'dra bar 
[\[357a.5\]]kha ra dkar por bskol zhing gdus pa las kha ra'i bye brag rnam pa gsum du 'byung ba steng rim gyis zhes bya ba dang /_bar nas 'byung ba ni li kha ra bzang por bya ba'i rgyu yin te/_hwags yin no/_/zhabs nas 'byung ba ni li kha ra nyid yin no/_/de la mthu'i bye brag ni steng pas 
[\[357a.6\]]bar gyi bzang /_bar bas nang gi zhabs bzang ste/_glo gcong dang /_glo rdol ba dang /_khrag dang mkhris pa'i nad rlung nad sel/_khong du phan te bud med la phod cing ro tsa bar 'gyur/_kha ra dang 'dra bar shing rlangs pa tsher ma can gyi khu ba bskol ba las kha ra byas pa yang li 
[\[357a.7\]]kha ra'i yon tan dang 'dra'o/_/li kha ra dkar po thams cad kyi yon tan ni tsha ba dang skom pa dang /_skyug bro ba dang brgyal ba dang khrag dang mkhris pa'i nad 'di rnams sel bar 'gyur ro/_/de la mdor bu ram shing las gdus te bu ram gyi dbu ba kha sbri sla bar 'ong ba lta bu ni ngan 
[\[357b\]]
[\[357b.1\]]no/_/bu ram shing yang rung kha ra'i shing yang rung ste/_de las byas pa'i kha ra dkar po ni bzang ste dge'o/_/de la sbrang rtsi ni mig la phan/_bad kan dang tshil che ba dang /_dbugs mi bde ba'i nad sel to/_/skom pa la phan/_dug sel/_skyigs bu dang khrag nad mkhris 
[\[357b.2\]]pa'i nad sel/_ha cang cho te sha che ba sha ran cing 'bri bar 'gyur/_mdze dang chu sri ba dang skyug bro ba dang /_dbugs mi bde ba dang /_lud pa lu ba dang /_tshad pa dang lto ba'i nang na srin bu'i gdug pa sel/_rma rnags pa rnag dang chu ser 'byin/_rma 'tsho/_chag grugs 
[\[357b.3\]]'byor/_rlung yang skye'o/_/mdor na sbrang rtsi ni mngar la ro bska/_khong du rtsub pa yin/_sbrang rtsi las khaN+Dar byas pa'i yon tan dang mthu yang de ltar 'dra'o/_/sbrang rtsi'i nyen ni ston dang dbyar sogs tsha ba'i dus la yang bab la tsha ba'i nad kyis kyang thebs la/_sman cha ba 
[\[357b.4\]]dang /_sbrang rtsi ni sbrang tshang nas phyung ma thag pa dron mo dang /_sman tsha bar gyur pa la 'di lta bur byas te btang na nad pa gsod do/_/dug gi me tog las sbrang rtsi byung ba dang /_sbrang ma gdug pas gsos pa ni dro ba yang rung grang ba yang rung ste mi rung ngo /_/skyug sman 
[\[357b.5\]]chu dron dang sbrang rtsi sbyar te btang ba'am/_sman mar mas gtong ba dron po'i nang du sbrang rtsi sbyar ba ni nyes pa med do/_/bu ram shing dang kha ra'i shing dang bu ram dang kha ra'i bye brag dang sbrang rtsi'i bye brag bshad pa rdzogs so//_//til mar dang e raN+Da'i mar dang 
[\[357b.6\]]yungs mar dang ba ru ra'i mar dang zar ma'i mar dang le brgan rtsi'i mar gyi phan gnod bstan pa/_til mar gyi phan gnod til dngos dang 'dra'o/_/de la 'bru mar gyi nang na til mar ni bzang ste/_til mar ni cung zad tsha ba 'thungs na lus kun la myur du khyab cing mched par 
[\[357b.7\]]byed/_nyen ni til mar 'ba' zhig rtag tu 'thungs na sha mdangs kyi pags pa'i mdog la gnod de sha bkra dang shu ba dang mdze lta bu'i nad du 'gyur ro/_/mig la gnod/_til mar dang sman dang /_sman gzhan du sbyar te btang ba ni nyen du mi 'ong ngo /_/til mar ni rno la tshan 
[\[358a\]]
[\[358a.1\]]che bas bad kan sel lo/_/skem pa la btang na sha skye/_sbom po tshon po la btang na sha grims par 'ong ngo /_/'khren pas thogs te rtug skam du 'ong ngo /_/lto ba nang gi srin bu sel to/_/til mar sman dang sbyar te btang na nad do cog thams cad 
[\[358a.2\]]sel to/_/e raN+Da dkar po'i mar ni kha la tsha zhing ro kha ba yin no/_/e raN+Da'i mar 'thungs na zhu ba'i rjes ni mdar bar 'gyur/_khong 'khru/_lci ste cung zad bsil bar 'gyur/_rgyu ma cher skyes te rlig rlugs su gyur pa'i nad dang /_skran dang rlung nad dang bad kan gyi nad dang /_
[\[358a.3\]]dmu rdzing la sogs pa gsus pa cher skyes pa'i nad dang /_rims nyin mtshan mi mthun par ldang ba'i nad dang /_'or gyi bar skran nad dang /_rked pa na ba dang /_mo mtshan pho mtshan na ba dang /_lto ba na ba dang /_rgyab na ba la phan/_e raN+Da dmar po'i mar ni rno la tsha/_sha rjen sungs pa'i 
[\[358a.4\]]dri rab tu mnam/_lag pas mnan na rab tu 'byil zhing 'dred/_phan gnod kyi yon tan ni e raN+Da dkar po bshad pa dang 'dra'o/_/yungs kar gyi mar ni ro tsha la drod che rno/_/yungs mar 'ba' zhig rtag tu 'thungs na bad kan dang rlung sel/_/bu chu 'bri bar 'gyur/_khong yad/_
[\[358a.5\]]mkhris pa dang khrag skye bar 'gyur te mi dge'o/_/phan pa ni rlung dang bad kan las gyur pa'i 'brum bu dang /_mdze dang gzhang 'brum gyi nad dang /_rma byung ba dang lto na srin bu yod pa dang /_'di rnams sel te phan no/_/ba ru ra'i mar ni ro mngar la bsil/[ {]glad pa[,]klad pa[}]ra bsku 
[\[358a.6\]]zhing sna nas btang na skra skye/_'ju dka' ste lci/_mkhris pa dang bad kan sel to/_/nim pa'i mar ni ro kha ste rab tu tsha zhing dro ba tsam ni ma yin/_lto'i srin bu dang mkhris pa dang bad kan gyi nad sel to/_/zar ma'i mar dang le brgan rtsi'i mar ni drod che/_sha mthang gi pags 
[\[358a.7\]]pa la gnod/_sha bkra thor bu shu ba mdze 'dra ba'i nad du 'gyur/_sman dang sbyar na ni de lta bu'i nyen du yang mi 'gyur/_bad kan dang mkhris pa skye'o/_/tshil dang rkang ni rlung nad sel to/_/nyams brta/stobs skye/_bad kan skye'o/_/lug dang ra'i tshil lta bu ni drod che bas 
[\[358b\]]
[\[358b.1\]]mkhris pa skye'o/_/bya na sha so so'i bye brag gi phan gnod ci 'dra ba de 'dra ba'i tshil yang de dang 'dra'o/_/bya na sha so so'i bye brag ni yul skam sa dang ser ches nas byung ba rnam pa gnyis te/_spyi'i yul skam sa dang ser che sa nas byung ba'i phan gnod bstan pa bzhin du

[\[399a.3\]]phyin pa'i rgyu rtsi sman dang /_gso ba'i thabs su sbyar zhing bya'o/_/de yang nad tshul du ma re re nas bstan mi nus te de ni 'dor cog thabs kyi nad dmigs ni rlung dang mkhris pa dang bad kan du 'dus so/_/dper nyin thog thag bya nam mkha' la 'phur te 'gro ste/_rang gi lus kyi 
[\[399a.4\]]grib ma dang 'bral du mi rung ba bzhin no/_/sems can no cog nad rnam pa gsum po chags pa dang rdul dang /_mun pa dang gsum du ldan pa las gyur pa'i nad rnam pa sna tshogs dang mi 'bral ba de ltar rang gi srog zungs bdun nang ma snyoms te nad du 'gyur ba'i rgyu yang chags pa dang 
[\[399a.5\]]rdul dang mun pa dang gsum gyis 'gyur bas/_rlung dang bad kan dang mkhris pa gsum nang ma 'phrod pa des srog zungs bdun po ma snyoms te 'khrugs na nad du 'gyur ro/_/nad du 'gyur ba de dag gi rgyu yang mdor rnam pa gsum ste/_yul dang dus dang 
[\[399a.6\]]las ngan par byed pa dang gsum mo/_/'di gsum po re re la yang chung ngu brten pa dang /_rab tu brten pa dang /_log par brten pa dang gsum du dbye'o/_/'di gsum po re re la yang chung ngu dang /_'bring po dang chen po gsum du phye na dgu dgur ro/_/de ji lta zhe na/_mig gi dbang po'i yul ni 
[\[399a.7\]]gzugs snang ba yin te/_yul de la gang bltar yang rung ste/_rang gi yul la mi lta ba'am/_yang na zhib tu legs par mi lta bar cung zad tsam mam/_'ol ji tsam zhig lta ba de lta bu ni/_gzugs mig gis lta ba chung bar sbyor ba zhes bya'o/_/gzugs la mig 
[\[399b\]]
[\[399b.1\]]gis ha cang yun ring du lta na de ni rab tu sbyor ba zhes bya ste/_de lta bu yang nad du 'gyur te mi rung ngo /_/yang gzugs chung ches pa la lta'am/_nyi ma lta bu'i lhang lhang po'i 'tsher ches pa la mig tshugs su lta ba'am/_gzugs 'jigs pa la lta'am/_mig gi drung nyid du/_
[\[399b.2\]]gzugs bzhag pa la lta'am/_yang na gzugs ha cang thag ring ches pa la rgyang mar mig tshugs su lta ba'am/_gzugs yid du 'ong ba la lta'am/_gzugs mi sdug pa la mig gis lta ba de lta bu log par lta ba'i rten pa zhes bya ste/_de lta bu yang nad du 'gyur te mi rung ngo /_/
[\[399b.3\]]de bzhin du rna ba la sogs pa'i yul sgra la sogs pa la yang rnam pa gsum gsum du blta ste/_rna ba'i yul sgra chen po drag po la nyan pa dang /_rul pa mnam po la snum pa la sogs pa lta bu yang mi rung ste/_so so'i dbang po yul gyi tshul du sbyar la/_chung ngu dang chen po dang log

[\[400a.1\]]las drag shul byed pa dang /_lus dang rkang lag ha cang gcus pa dang /_rdeg 'cha' ba dang /_gom pa 'khyor bzhin 'thub pa 'di ni log pa'i sbyor ba zhes bya ste lus la gnod do/_/ngag gi smra ba yang tshul de dang 'dra ste/_kha nas zas ldad bzhin du

[\[408a.2\]]bu la lus brta ba'i cho ga bya'o/_/de la lus brta ba ni sha dang 'o ma dang /_mar dang /_mngar bag dang /_snum bag dang /_li kha ra dkar po dang /_sman mas gtong ba 'di dag gis khong brta zhing nyams 'tshag bskyed la/_yang lus la phan pa mtshan mo mal na gnyid bde bar log par bya/_lus la 
[\[408a.3\]]mar gyis byug cing mnyes te/_chu dron pos khrus bya ba dang /_nyams bag tu bde bar 'dug pa dang /_nyams dga' bar bya ba 'di lta bus lus brta'o/_/yang lus kyi sha 'tshag nyams smad cing dbri bar bya ba ni/_gcin snyi ba'i nad dang /_pho ba'i nang be'u snabs yod de mchin pa na ba dang /_zas snum bag 
[\[408a.4\]]ha cang zos sam/_lus kyi khong snum ches sam/_rims kyis btab bam/_brla rengs pa'i nad yod dam/_mdzes btab bam/_shu chen mched pa'am/_khong 'bras dang /_mcher pa na ba dang /_klad pa na ba dang /_lkog ma na ba dang /_mig na ba dang /_lus sha bskyed de sbom por 'gyur ba dang /_'di 
[\[408a.5\]]lta bu dang /_dgun stod kyi tshe lta bu rtag tu zas bsnams shing kha zas dbri ba dang /_smyung bar bya'o/_/de la sman gyis rtag tu bkru zhing sbyang ba ni lus ha cang sha che zhing spom pa dang /_nyams stobs che ba'i mi dang /_tsha ba mkhris pa rgyas pa'i mi dang /_bad kan rgyas te che ba dang /_pho ba'i nang na be'u 
[\[408a.6\]]snabs yod pas zas mi 'ju ste mchin pa na ba dang /_rims na ba dang /_rtag tu slon zhing skyug bro ba dang /_tshad pa rab tu 'khru ba'i nad dang /_snying na ba dang /_bshang gci 'gags pa ste 'ong du mi rung ba dang /_lus dang khong lci ba dang /_sgregs pa rtag tu 'ong ba dang /_yi ga 'chus shing dong ga mi 
[\[408a.7\]]bde ba la sogs pa la/_bkru sman dang skyug sman drag tu cher btang ngo /_/sman gyis ran par bkru zhing sbyang ba 'bring tsam du btang ba'i dmigs ni/_sha che chung ran tsam gyi mi la ni sngar nad bzhu ba'i sman btang drod bskyed la/_de'i rjes la nad kyi dmigs gong ma ltar mi bde zhing na ba gang 
[\[408b\]]
[\[408b.1\]]yang rung ste/_de lta bu'i mi dang /_yang la la sha chung zhing skem la nad dmigs gong ma ltar na ba de rnams la/_bkru zhing sbyang ba'i sman drag dal ran pa 'bring tsam du btang ba dang /_nyam chung zhing rke ba la thun chung zhing 'jam por btang la/_de'i tshe na kha zas bsdam zhing skom thub par bya'o/_/
[\[408b.2\]]yong ni nad pa'i nyams stobs 'bring tsam cig la mi bde na/_de la dpyad bkru sman gyis bkru zhing sbyang mi dgos te/_de lta bu ni lus bsig cing bcag pa dang /_nad dmigs dang sbyar te/_rlung dang ser bu las bsang ba gar legs par bya'o/_/nyams 'tshag 'bring la yang bkru sman 
[\[408b.3\]]la sogs pa mi btang na/_nad pa nyam chung ba la bkru sman 'jam po yang btang du mi rung ba lta smos kyang ci dgos so/_/nad pa lus kyi nyams 'tshag smad cing bri ba lta bu nyams 'tshag gso zhing brta bar bya ba'i mi la ni/_zas bsdams pa dang /_smyung ba la sogs pa cung zad tsam byar 
[\[408b.4\]]rung ngo /_/spyir gso zhing nyams 'tshag brta ba'i dpyad bya ba dang skyug sman dang bkru sman dang smyung bar byas te/_nyams 'tshag smad par bya ba yang yul dang stobs dang /_dus dang /_mi'i na tshod dang /_nyams 'tshag dang sbyar te/_ha cang nyams 'bri ba dang /_ha cang nyams brta ba yang mi bya'o/_/
[\[408b.5\]]de la lus kyi nyams 'tshag brta ba'i dpyad bya ste/_nyams mthu brtas par 'gyur bar mngon zhing nyams 'tshag brtas/_sha skyes stobs dang ldan par gyur na/_mig dang rna ba la sogs pa dbang po rnams gsal/_bshang gci'i dri ma mi sri bar 'byung /_lus khong yang zhing 
[\[408b.6\]]bshor/_kha zas yi gar 'ong na de lta bu nad med par gso zhing cho ga byar rung ngo /_/yang kha zas bsdam zhing dbri ba dang smyung bar byas na/_phan par mngon pa'i rtags ni kha zas phra zhing bsdams sam/_smyung bar byas pa las kha zas zhu ste/_bkres shing skom par 'gyur ba dang /_khong gsud pa'i 
[\[408b.7\]]sgregs pa mnam po mi zhim pa med pa tha mal bar 'byung la khong ba'i nang sang sang por gyur la sems kyang gsal/_dang ga dang gre ba yang sang sang por gyur la/_na ba dang mi bde ba yang rtse zhil la/_khong yang zhing ci bya'o cog la sems spro ba yod/_myo myo po snyom pa'i bag med pa de lta bu ni kha zas

[\[409a.1\]]smyung ba byas pas phan par mngon pa yin no/_/nyams 'tshag brta ste sha bskyed pa'i cho ga tshod ma zin te ha cang drags pas sha ha cang cher skyes sam/_kha zas bri zhing smad pa dang /_smyung bar byas pa drags pas ha cang skem por gyur pa 'di gnyis lta bu la sman dpyad kyi cho 
[\[409a.2\]]gar bya ba ni/_sha cher skyes pa dang /_kha zas smyung bar byas pa las nad cir gyur pa'i tshul mngon te/_lus sha ha cang che zhing tsho ba las ni nad 'brum bu dang /_gcin snyi ba'i nad dang /_rims nad dang /_dmu rdzing lta bu gsus pa cher skyes pa'i nad dang /_bshang gci 'byung ba'i mtshams 
[\[409a.3\]]su mtshan bar rdol ba'i nad dang /_lud pa lu ba dang /_rims rmongs bus 'debs pa dang /_gcin sri ba dang pho bar be'u snabs chags te zas mi 'ju ba dang /_mdze la sogs pa'i nad mi bzad pa rnams 'byung ngo /_/de la sha che zhing tshon po ni tshil zad par bya ba dang /_rlung dang bad kan 
[\[409a.4\]]bsal bar bya ba lta bu'i dpyang thams cad bzang ngo /_/de la mi tshon po skem po grims par bya ba'i kha zas ni/_rgya sran ku lang+tha dang /_dzur na dang /_sha ma ka khre rgod dang /_nas dang /_sran chung mon sran rde'u dang /_'di rnams g.yos su byas pa bzang zhing /_skom du ni sbrang rtsi chu dang sbyar ba 
[\[409a.5\]]dang /_zho ga chu dang /_dar ba dang 'di rnams btung ngo /_/yang sman a ri ta'i khu ba skom du bsnan pa btung ngo /_/sems las bskyed pa dang /_skyug sman dang bkru sman btang ba dang /_lus bsig cing bcag pa dang /_mtshan mo gdod mi nyal bar bya'o/_/sman ni a ru ra dang /_ba ru ra dang /_skyu ru ra'i 'bras 
[\[409a.6\]]bu gsum zhib tu btags la/_sbrang rtsi'i 'de gus sbrus te bza'o/_/yang sman gu ru ce sle tres dang /_a ru ra dang /_gla sgang chen po 'di gsum btags te sbrang rtsi la sbrus pa bza'o/_/yang sman ra tsan dza na zhes bya ba skyer khaN+Da dang /_rtsa ba chen po lnga zhes bya ba sa la par+Ni dang /_kr-iSh+Na par+Ni dang /_
[\[409a.7\]]bri ha ti dang /_kaN+Da ka ri dang /_gze ma dang 'di lnga btang ngo /_/yang gu gul btang ba'i dpyad dang /_sman brag zhun btang ba'i dpyad bya'o/_/sman ag+ni man+tha'i khu ba yang btang na yang sha 'bri'o/_/yang sman byi dang ka dang /_sga dang /_ya bak+Sha ra nas tshig 'khus pa'i tsha dang /_lcags kyi cha tsha phye ma nag po dang /_nas dang /_
